# Screenshot Editing Sources

These PNGs preserve the full dimensions of the retained captures, with account identities, private native URLs and mailbox surroundings masked. They have not been cropped or resized during this export. Unredacted originals stay local.

The source library contains **216 application-window captures, six focused captures and six PDF-page renders**. Three early images have no retained original. A full application capture contains the browser application viewport, not necessarily browser tabs, the address bar or the Windows desktop.

Participant notes continue to use the existing approved crops. Inspect the whole frame before replacing a lesson image: surrounding content may fall outside the illustrated step. Two procedure-plan sources explicitly need an earlier-stage recapture before whole-frame use.

## Find and Adjust an Image

The table links each lesson image to its retained source. The [manifest](./manifest.json) records dimensions, source type, privacy masks and the approved crop rectangle where it was retained. Coordinates use the original PNG pixels.

Use Pillow to render an adjusted crop without editing the source:

```text
python _design/screenshots/render-crop.py --image 05-sheets-advanced/01-source-audit/images/c05-01-01-empty-advanced-folder.jpg --box 0 0 1432 781 --output preview.jpg
```

Review the output before replacing a participant image or rebuilding books.

Google Docs and Google Slides lesson images are no longer hand-cropped. They are drawn from these sources by `annotate-shots`; see [`_design/shots/README.md`](../shots/README.md).

## Foundation 5: Validation and Focused Views

Foundation Lab 5 adds 34 staged captures: strict canonical status rules and actual Done rejection, planned status/week matrix, whole-week custom formula accepting 1/4 and rejecting 0/5/1.5, clearing limitations, restored practice week 1, entry guide, two saved bounded filter views with 8/2 checked IDs, and ordinary SUM 10400 before/during filtering. Views are closed with all 12 source rows visible; source values/formulas and original practice records are unchanged. Independent Procurement/coverage repair and learner timing remain pending. Programme 570 images; retained 567 sources.

### 29 October 2026: AI7 author evaluation

AI7 author reference: predefined five-topic rubric and three-source pack; A first review 4/5 with an omitted sponsor-decision detail, then author correction checked 5/5; fresh reordered B first review 5/5. The initial question/rubric mismatch is recorded as a design limitation, not a false-date assertion. Targeted revised-prompt C was submitted and showed generation after more than seven minutes, then returned to the new-chat composer with its prompt restored and no answer. No cause was established; no retry was submitted. It remains unscored. Two completed assisted outputs and no human manual baseline do not establish productivity gain, causal prompt improvement or independent learner performance. Programme 1,175 images; 1,172 retained originals; 99 AI captures. Two AI routes still lack staged screenshots: Lab 6 has a written reference, and Lab 8 is not executed. Human/classroom timing and independent/reviewer checks remain pending.

## Lossless optimization

All 1,198 retained PNGs and 1,201 lesson JPEGs were checked during the final lossless compression pass. Dimensions and decoded pixels are preserved. See the [optimization report](../../planning/IMAGE-OPTIMIZATION.md) for measured savings and verification.
